```html
          <input type="number" id="shortInput" min="1" max="60" required />
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
          <label for="longInput">Long Break (minutes)</label>
          <input type="number" id="longInput" min="1" max="60" required />

        <div class="modal-actions">
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-save">Save</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // --- Application State ---
    const config = {
      work: 25,
      shortBreak: 5,
      longBreak: 15,
      longBreakInterval: 4
    };

    let currentMode = 'work'; // 'work', 'shortBreak', 'longBreak'
    let isRunning = false;
    let timerId = null;
    let targetTime = null;
    let remainingSeconds = config.work * 60;
    let totalSeconds = config.work * 60;
    let workCycleCount = 1;

    // --- DOM Elements ---
    const timeDisplay = document.getElementById('timeDisplay');
    const sessionLabel = document.getElementById('sessionLabel');
    const startBtn = document.getElementById('startBtn');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetBtn');
    const skipBtn = document.getElementById('skipBtn');
    const modeButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn');
    const progressRing = document.getElementById('progressRing');
    const alertBanner = document.getElementById('alertBanner');
    const alertMessage = document.getElementById('alertMessage');
    
    // Modal Elements
    const modalOverlay = document.getElementById('modalOverlay');
    const openSettingsBtn = document.getElementById('openSettingsBtn');
    const closeSettingsBtn = document.getElementById('closeSettingsBtn');
    const settingsForm = document.getElementById('settingsForm');
    const workInput = document.getElementById('workInput');
    const shortInput = document.getElementById('shortInput');
    const longInput = document.getElementById('longInput');

    // --- Progress Ring Configuration ---
    const radius = progressRing.r.baseVal.value;
    const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
    progressRing.style.strokeDasharray = `${circumference} ${circumference}`;
    progressRing.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';

    function setProgress(percent) {
      const offset = circumference - (percent / 100) * circumference;
      progressRing.style.strokeDashoffset = offset;
    }

    // --- Synthetic Audio Notification ---
    // Uses the Web Audio API to create a gentle chime without external audio files.
    function playBeep() {
      try {
        const audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;

        // Two-tone bell chord
        [523.25, 659.25].forEach((freq, idx) => {
          const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
          const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
          
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + idx * 0.12);
          
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, now + idx * 0.12);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 1.2);

          osc.connect(gain);
          gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);

          osc.start(now + idx * 0.12);
          osc.stop(now + 1.2);
        });
      } catch (e) {
        // Fallback silently if autoplay audio policies block playback
      }
    }

    // --- Visual Alert ---
    let alertTimeout;
    function triggerVisualAlert(message) {
      playBeep();
      document.body.classList.add('flashing');
      alertMessage.textContent = message;
      alertBanner.classList.add('show');

      clearTimeout(alertTimeout);
      alertTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
        document.body.classList.remove('flashing');
        alertBanner.classList.remove('show');
      }, 5000);
    }

    // --- Timer Core ---
    function formatTime(seconds) {
      const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
      const secs = seconds % 60;
      return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
    }

    function updateDisplay() {
      timeDisplay.textContent = formatTime(remainingSeconds);
      const progress = ((totalSeconds - remainingSeconds) / totalSeconds) * 100;
      setProgress(progress);

      const modeNames = { work: 'Work', shortBreak: 'Short Break', longBreak: 'Long Break' };
      document.title = `(${formatTime(remainingSeconds)}) ${modeNames[currentMode]} - Pomodoro`;
    }

    function tick() {
      const now = Date.now();
      remainingSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.round((targetTime - now) / 1000));
      updateDisplay();

      if (remainingSeconds <= 0) {
        clearInterval(timerId);
        timerId = null;
        isRunning = false;
        handleSessionCompletion();
      }
    }

    function startTimer() {
      if (isRunning) return;
      isRunning = true;
      startBtn.textContent = 'PAUSE';
      targetTime = Date.now() + remainingSeconds * 1000;
      timerId = setInterval(tick, 200);
    }

    function pauseTimer() {
      if (!isRunning) return;
      isRunning = false;
      startBtn.textContent = 'RESUME';
      clearInterval(timerId);
      timerId = null;
    }

    function resetTimer() {
      pauseTimer();
      startBtn.textContent = 'START';
      remainingSeconds = config[currentMode] * 60;
      totalSeconds = remainingSeconds;
      updateDisplay();
    }

    function setMode(mode) {
      currentMode = mode;
      
      // Update UI Theme
      const colors = {
        work: 'var(--work-bg)',
        shortBreak: 'var(--short-bg)',
        longBreak: 'var(--long-bg)'
      };
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--current-theme', colors[mode]);

      modeButtons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.mode === mode);
      });

      sessionLabel.textContent = (mode === 'work') 
        ? `Session #${workCycleCount}` 
        : (mode === 'shortBreak' ? 'Short Break' : 'Long Break');

      resetTimer();
    }

    function handleSessionCompletion() {
      if (currentMode === 'work') {
        if (workCycleCount % config.longBreakInterval === 0) {
          triggerVisualAlert("Work interval finished! Time for a Long Break.");
          setMode('longBreak');
        } else {
          triggerVisualAlert("Work interval finished! Time for a Short Break.");
          setMode('shortBreak');
        }
      } else {
        triggerVisualAlert("Break complete! Ready to start working?");
        if (currentMode === 'longBreak') {
          workCycleCount = 1;
        } else {
          workCycleCount++;
        }
        setMode('work');
      }
    }

    function skipSession() {
      pauseTimer();
      handleSessionCompletion();
    }

    // --- Modal / Settings Handling ---
    function openSettings() {
      workInput.value = config.work;
      shortInput.value = config.shortBreak;
      longInput.value = config.longBreak;
      modalOverlay.classList.add('active');
    }

    function closeSettings() {
      modalOverlay.classList.remove('active');
    }

    settingsForm.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      config.work = parseInt(workInput.value, 10);
      config.shortBreak = parseInt(shortInput.value, 10);
      config.longBreak = parseInt(longInput.value, 10);
      closeSettings();
      resetTimer();
    });

    // --- Event Listeners ---
    startBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (isRunning) {
        pauseTimer();
      } else {
        startTimer();
      }
    });

    resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetTimer);
    skipBtn.addEventListener('click', skipSession);

    modeButtons.forEach(button => {
      button.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        setMode(e.target.dataset.mode);
      });
    });

    openSettingsBtn.addEventListener('click', openSettings);
    closeSettingsBtn.addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
    modalOverlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === modalOverlay) closeSettings();
    });

    // Initialize display
    setMode('work');
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

### Key Features Included:
1. **Configurable Intervals**: Click **⚙ Settings** to customize Work, Short Break, and Long Break durations in minutes. Changes update in real-time.
2. **Accurate Timing Engine**: Uses timestamp deltas (`Date.now() - targetTime`) instead of simple, uncalibrated `setInterval` increments, ensuring the timer stays accurate even if the browser tab is backgrounded.
3. **Controls**: Start, Pause, Resume, Reset, and Skip session buttons.
4. **Visual Alerts**:
   - A pulsing brightness animation across the viewport when an interval finishes.
   - An alert banner notifying the user that the session has switched.
   - Live document title countdown updating (e.g., `(24:45) Work - Pomodoro`).
   - Built-in two-tone audio chime using the native Web Audio API (no external sound files required).
5. **Responsive Design**: Mobile-friendly centered card UI with fluid SVG circular progress ring and responsive styling. Zero external CSS/JS dependencies.

  #btnSound.muted .ic-off{ display:block; }

  /* ---------- Stage ---------- */
  .stage{
    flex:1; display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
    padding:clamp(28px,6vh,56px) 0; position:relative; text-align:center;
  }
  .phase-row{ display:flex; align-items:center; gap:16px; flex-wrap:wrap; justify-content:center; }
  .stamp{
    display:inline-block; font-size:11px; font-weight:700; line-height:1;
    letter-spacing:.24em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--phase);
    border:1.5px solid currentColor; border-radius:3px; padding:8px 12px 7px 14px;
    transform:rotate(-2deg); transition:color .4s;
  }
  .count{
    font-size:11px; letter-spacing:.2em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--muted);
  }

  .clock{ position:relative; display:grid; place-items:center; margin-top:clamp(16px,4vh,36px); user-select:none; }
  .clock-time{
    grid-area:1/1;
    font-weight:700; font-size:clamp(4.2rem, 17.5vw, 10rem); line-height:.95; letter-spacing:-.04em;
    color:var(--ink); transition:color .4s, opacity .3s;
  }
  .clock-time.fill{ color:var(--phase); will-change:clip-path; }
  .clock.running .colon{ animation:blink 1.1s steps(1) infinite; }
  .clock.paused .clock-time{ opacity:.4; }
  @keyframes blink{ 0%,54%{opacity:1} 55%,100%{opacity:.12} }
  .clock.pulse .clock-time{ animation:pulse .9s ease; }
  @keyframes pulse{ 0%{transform:scale(1)} 18%{transform:scale(1.045)} 40%{transform:scale(1)} 60%{transform:scale(1.025)} 100%{transform:scale(1)} }

  .hint{
    margin-top:clamp(14px,3vh,28px); min-height:1.5em;
    font-family:'Fraunces', Georgia, serif; font-style:italic;
    font-size:clamp(15px,2.4vw,19px); color:var(--muted);
    transition:opacity .22s; padding:0 16px;
  }

  /* Banner stamp slapped over the clock on transitions */
  .banner{
    position:absolute; left:50%; top:44%; z-index:6; pointer-events:none; white-space:nowrap;
    background:var(--paper); border:2px solid var(--phase); color:var(--phase);
    padding:12px 22px; border-radius:4px;
    font-family:'Fraunces', Georgia, serif; font-style:italic; font-weight:600;
    font-size:clamp(17px,3vw,22px);
    box-shadow:0 10px 28px rgba(35,28,15,.14);
    opacity:0; transform:translate(-50%,-50%) rotate(-2deg) scale(.72);
    transition:opacity .25s, transform .3s cubic-bezier(.2,1.5,.4,1);
  }
  .banner.show{ opacity:1; transform:translate(-50%,-50%) rotate(-2deg) scale(1); }

  /* ---------- Controls ---------- */
  .controls{ display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; margin-top:clamp(26px,5vh,44px); }
  .btn-main{
    display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:11px;
    min-width:176px; padding:15px 30px;
    background:var(--ink); color:var(--paper); border:1px solid var(--ink); border-radius:9px;
    font-size:13px; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:.22em; text-transform:uppercase; cursor:pointer;
    transition:background .2s, border-color .2s, transform .06s;
  }
  .btn-main:hover{ background:var(--phase); border-color:var(--phase); }
  .btn-main:active{ transform:translateY(1px); }
  .icon-btn.ctl{ width:50px; height:50px; border-radius:9px; }

  /* ---------- Ribbon (session timeline) ---------- */
  .ribbon-wrap{ border-top:1px solid var(--line); padding:18px 0 22px; }
  .ribbon{ position:relative; display:flex; gap:4px; height:16px; margin:9px 0 11px; }
  .seg{
    position:relative; flex-basis:0; min-width:5px;
    border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:2px; overflow:hidden;
    transform-origin:left;
  }
  .ribbon.animate .seg{ animation:segIn .5s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1) both; }
  @keyframes segIn{ from{ transform:scaleX(0); } }
  .seg i{ position:absolute; inset:0; width:0%; display:block; transition:width .3s linear; }
  .seg-work  i{ background:var(--tomato); }
  .seg-short i{ background:var(--sage); }
  .seg-long  i{ background:var(--spruce); }
  .playhead{
    position:absolute; top:-6px; bottom:-6px; width:2px; margin-left:-1px;
    background:var(--ink); border-radius:1px; transition:left .2s linear;
  }
  .playhead::after{
    content:""; position:absolute; top:-5px; left:50%; width:6px; height:6px;
    background:var(--ink); transform:translateX(-50%) rotate(45deg);
  }
  .cap{
    display:flex; justify-content:space-between; gap:12px;
    font-size:11px; letter-spacing:.14em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--muted);
  }

  /* ---------- Flash alert ---------- */
  #flash{ position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:80; pointer-events:none; opacity:0; }
  #flash.go{ animation:flash 1.3s linear; }
  @keyframes flash{
    0%{opacity:0} 7%{opacity:.95} 20%{opacity:0}
    33%{opacity:.75} 46%{opacity:0} 60%{opacity:.45} 74%{opacity:0} 100%{opacity:0}
  }

  /* ---------- Settings drawer ---------- */
  .scrim{
    position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:90; background:rgba(30,25,17,.4);
    opacity:0; pointer-events:none; transition:opacity .3s;
  }
  .scrim.open{ opacity:1; pointer-events:auto; }
  .drawer{
    position:fixed; top:0; right:0; bottom:0; z-index:95;
    width:min(380px,94vw); background:var(--paper); border-left:1px solid var(--line);
    display:flex; flex-direction:column;
    transform:translateX(103%); visibility:hidden;
    transition:transform .38s cubic-bezier(.22,.9,.3,1), visibility 0s .38s;
  }
  .drawer.open{ transform:none; visibility:visible; transition:transform .38s cubic-bezier(.22,.9,.3,1); }
  .drawer-head{
    display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between;
    padding:20px 22px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .drawer-head h2{ font-family:'Fraunces', Georgia, serif; font-style:italic; font-weight:600; font-size:22px; }
  .drawer-body{ flex:1; overflow-y:auto; padding:6px 22px; }
  .field, .switch-row{
    display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:14px;
    padding:17px 0; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .field-label span{ display:block; font-family:'Fraunces', Georgia, serif; font-weight:500; font-size:17px; }
  .field-label em{
    display:block; margin-top:5px; font-style:normal;
    font-size:10px; letter-spacing:.16em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--muted);
  }
  .stepper{ display:flex; align-items:center; gap:3px; flex:none; }
  .step-btn{
    width:32px; height:38px; border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:6px;
    background:transparent; color:var(--ink); font-size:17px; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer;
    transition:background .15s, color .15s; user-select:none; touch-action:none;
  }
  .step-btn:hover{ background:var(--ink); color:var(--paper); }
  .stepper input{
    width:52px; height:38px; text-align:center; border:none; border-bottom:1.5px solid var(--ink);
    background:transparent; color:var(--ink); font-family:inherit; font-size:17px; font-weight:700;
    appearance:textfield;
  }
  .stepper input:focus{ outline:none; border-bottom-color:var(--phase); }
  .switch-row{ cursor:pointer; }
  .switch{ position:relative; width:44px; height:24px; flex:none; }
  .switch input{ position:absolute; opacity:0; width:100%; height:100%; cursor:pointer; }
  .switch i{ position:absolute; inset:0; background:var(--line); border-radius:99px; transition:background .2s; pointer-events:none; }
  .switch i::after{
    content:""; position:absolute; top:3px; left:3px; width:18px; height:18px; border-radius:50%;
    background:var(--paper); box-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.3); transition:transform .2s;
  }
  .switch input:checked + i{ background:var(--ink); }
  .switch input:checked + i::after{ transform:translateX(20px); }
  .drawer-foot{
    padding:16px 22px; border-top:1px solid var(--line);
    display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px 18px; align-items:center;
    font-size:10px; letter-spacing:.12em; text-transform:uppercase; color:var(--muted);
  }
  kbd{
    border:1px solid var(--line); border-bottom-width:2px; border-radius:4px;
    padding:2px 6px; font:inherit; color:var(--ink);
  }

  /* ---------- Toast ---------- */
  .toast{
    position:fixed; left:50%; bottom:26px; z-index:120; transform:translate(-50%,12px);
    background:var(--ink); color:var(--paper); padding:11px 18px; border-radius:6px;
    font-size:12px; letter-spacing:.08em; opacity:0; pointer-events:none;
    transition:opacity .25s, transform .25s;
  }
  .toast.show{ opacity:1; transform:translate(-50%,0); }

  /* ---------- Entrance ---------- */
  .rise{ animation:rise .7s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1) both; }
  @keyframes rise{ from{ opacity:0; transform:translateY(16px); } }

  @media (max-width:560px){
    .wordmark{ font-size:22px; }
    .btn-main{ flex:1; }
    .cap{ font-size:10px; letter-spacing:.1em; }
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
    *{ animation-duration:.01ms !important; animation-iteration-count:1 !important; transition-duration:.01ms !important; }
  }
</style>
</head>

<script>
(function(){
  'use strict';

  /* ---------- Constants & state ---------- */
  var $ = function(s){ return document.querySelector(s); };
  var KEY = 'pomodoro.v1';
  var DEFAULTS = { focus:25, short:5, long:15, rounds:4, autoStart:true, sound:true };
  var settings = (function(){
    try{ return Object.assign({}, DEFAULTS, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)) || {}); }
    catch(e){ return Object.assign({}, DEFAULTS); }
  })();

  var PHASES = {
    work:  { name:'Focus',       color:'#C13A17' },
    short: { name:'Short break', color:'#4F7A57' },
    long:  { name:'Long break',  color:'#2C5747' }
  };
  var HINTS = {
    idle:  ['Ready when you are.','Twenty-five minutes. One tomato.','Set the dial. Take a breath.'],
    work:  ['One tomato at a time.','Stay with it. The clock is kind.','Deep work, gentle clock.','Ignore the pings. Trust the timer.'],
    short: ['Stand up. Look at something far away.','Water. Stretch. Unclench your jaw.','Let the mind drift for a bit.'],
    long:  ['A longer rest. You earned every minute.','Step away from the screen for a while.','Walk, breathe, return.']
  };
  var ICONS = {
    play:  '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 4.5v15l13-7.5z"/></svg>',
    pause: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 5h4v14H6zM14 5h4v14h-4z"/></svg>'
  };

  var blocks = [];          // [{type, dur(seconds)}]
  var current = 0;          // index of active block
  var remaining = 0;        // ms left in active block
  var state = 'idle';       // idle | running | paused
  var endAt = 0;            // performance.now() timestamp when running
  var segEls = [];

  /* ---------- Elements ---------- */
  var timeBase = $('#timeBase'), timeFill = $('#timeFill'), clockEl = $('#clock');
  var stampEl = $('#stamp'), countEl = $('#count'), hintEl = $('#hint');
  var btnMain = $('#btnMain'), btnReset = $('#btnReset'), btnSkip = $('#btnSkip');
  var ribbonEl = $('#ribbon'), playhead = $('#playhead');
  var capL = $('#capL'), capR = $('#capR');
  var flashEl = $('#flash'), bannerEl = $('#banner'), toastEl = $('#toast');
  var drawer = $('#drawer'), scrim = $('#scrim');
  var btnSound = $('#btnSound'), optAuto = $('#optAuto'), optSound = $('#optSound');
  var favLink = $('#favicon'), favCanvas = null;

  /* ---------- Helpers ---------- */
  function clamp(v, lo, hi){ return Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v)); }
  function pad(n){ return String(n).padStart(2,'0'); }
  function fmt(ms){
    var s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms/1000));
    return pad(Math.floor(s/60)) + ':' + pad(s%60);
  }
  function fmtTotal(ms){
    var s = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms/1000));
    if (s < 3600) return fmt(s*1000);
    return Math.floor(s/3600) + ':' + pad(Math.floor(s%3600/60)) + ':' + pad(s%60);
  }
  function totalMs(){ return blocks.reduce(function(a,b){ return a + b.dur*1000; }, 0); }
  function elapsedMs(){
    var done = 0;
    for (var i=0; i<current; i++) done += blocks[i].dur*1000;
    return done + (blocks[current].dur*1000 - remaining);
  }
  function save(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(settings)); }catch(e){} }

  /* ---------- Schedule / ribbon ---------- */
  function buildSchedule(){
    blocks = [];
    for (var i=1; i<=settings.rounds; i++){
      blocks.push({ type:'work', dur: settings.focus*60 });
      var last = (i === settings.rounds);
      blocks.push({ type: last ? 'long' : 'short', dur: (last ? settings.long : settings.short)*60 });
    }
  }
  function buildRibbon(animate){
    ribbonEl.querySelectorAll('.seg').forEach(function(el){ el.remove(); });
    segEls = [];
    blocks.forEach(function(b, i){
      var s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'seg seg-' + b.type;
      s.style.flexGrow = b.dur;                 // width proportional to duration
      if (animate) s.style.animationDelay = (0.35 + i*0.05) + 's';
      s.appendChild(document.createElement('i'));
      ribbonEl.insertBefore(s, playhead);
      segEls.push(s);
    });
    if (animate){
      ribbonEl.classList.add('animate');
      setTimeout(function(){ ribbonEl.classList.remove('animate'); }, 1600);
    }
  }

  /* ---------- Rendering ---------- */
  function timeHTML(ms){
    var s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms/1000));
    return pad(Math.floor(s/60)) + '<span class="colon">:</span>' + pad(s%60);
  }
  function renderDynamic(){
    var t = timeHTML(remaining);
    timeBase.innerHTML = t;
    timeFill.innerHTML = t;

    var dur = blocks[current].dur*1000;
    var p = dur ? clamp(1 - remaining/dur, 0, 1) : 0;
    timeFill.style.clipPath = 'inset(' + ((1-p)*100).toFixed(2) + '% 0 0 0)';

    segEls.forEach(function(el, i){
      el.firstChild.style.width = (i < current ? 100 : i === current ? p*100 : 0) + '%';
    });
    playhead.style.left = (elapsedMs()/totalMs()*100) + '%';

    capL.textContent = fmt(elapsedMs()) + ' elapsed';
    capR.textContent = fmtTotal(totalMs()) + ' total';
    document.title = state === 'idle'
      ? 'Pomodoro'
      : fmt(remaining) + ' \u00B7 ' + PHASES[blocks[current].type].name;
  }
  function renderPhase(){
    var ph = blocks[current].type;
    document.body.dataset.phase = ph;
    stampEl.textContent = PHASES[ph].name;
    countEl.textContent = 'Block ' + (current+1) + ' of ' + blocks.length;
    updateFavicon();
  }
  function renderControls(){
    var running = state === 'running';
    var midSession = current > 0 || remaining < blocks[current].dur*1000;
    var label = running ? 'Pause' : (midSession ? 'Resume' : 'Start');
    btnMain.innerHTML = (running ? ICONS.pause : ICONS.play) + '<span>' + label + '</span>';
    clockEl.classList.toggle('running', running);
    clockEl.classList.toggle('paused', state === 'paused');
  }
  function renderAll(){ renderPhase(); renderControls(); renderDynamic(); }

  function pickHint(animate){
    var key = state === 'idle' ? 'idle' : blocks[current].type;
    var list = HINTS[key];
    var next = list[Math.floor(Math.random()*list.length)];
    if (!animate){ hintEl.textContent = next; return; }
    hintEl.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(function(){ hintEl.textContent = next; hintEl.style.opacity = 1; }, 220);
  }

  /* ---------- Favicon (tiny live detail) ---------- */
  function updateFavicon(){
    if (!favCanvas) favCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    favCanvas.width = favCanvas.height = 64;
    var g = favCanvas.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = '#F4EFE4'; g.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
    g.fillStyle = state === 'running' ? PHASES[blocks[current].type].color : '#221E18';
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(32,32,16,0,Math.PI*2); g.fill();
    favLink.href = favCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  }

  /* ---------- Sound (WebAudio, generated locally) ---------- */
  var ACtx = null;
  function ensureAudio(){
    if (!settings.sound) return null;
    if (!ACtx){
      try{ ACtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); }
      catch(e){ return null; }
    }
    if (ACtx.state === 'suspended') ACtx.resume();
    return ACtx;
  }
  function note(a, f, at, dur, vol){
    var o = a.createOscillator(), g = a.createGain();
    o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.value = f;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, at);

    o.connect(g); g.connect(a.destination);

    var a = ensureAudio(); if (!a) return;
    var t = a.currentTime + 0.03;

      if (state === 'running') endAt = performance.now() + remaining;

# Pomodoro Timer — Plain HTML, CSS & JavaScript

No dependencies are needed here: the app is a single-page timer with DOM updates, which browsers handle natively via `setInterval`/`Date`, CSS animations for alerts, and the built-in `Audio` API. Adding a framework would only bloat it.

```html
<script>
"use strict";

// ---------- State ----------
const state = {
  mode: "work",           // "work" | "break" | "longBreak"
  totalSeconds: 25 * 60,  // total for current interval
  remainingMs: 25 * 60 * 1000,
  running: false,
  completedSessions: 0,
  lastTick: null,
  rafId: null,
};

// ---------- Elements ----------
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const els = {
  app: $("app"),
  badge: $("modeBadge"),
  time: $("timeDisplay"),
  ring: $("ringProgress"),
  sessions: $("sessionCount"),
  startPause: $("startPauseBtn"),
  skip: $("skipBtn"),
  reset: $("resetBtn"),
  work: $("workInput"),
  brk: $("breakInput"),
  long: $("longInput"),
  longInterval: $("longIntervalInput"),
  autoStart: $("autoStart"),
};

// Ring geometry
const RADIUS = 52;
const CIRCUMFERENCE = 2 * Math.PI * RADIUS;
els.ring.style.strokeDasharray = CIRCUMFERENCE;

// ---------- Timing ----------
// Uses timestamps instead of counting intervals, so the timer
// stays accurate even if the tab is throttled by the browser.
function tick(now) {
  if (!state.running) return;
  state.remainingMs -= (now - state.lastTick);
  state.lastTick = now;

  if (state.remainingMs <= 0) {
    state.remainingMs = 0;
    render();
    intervalFinished();
    return;
  }
  render();
  state.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

function start() {
  if (state.running) return;
  state.running = true;
  state.lastTick = performance.now();
  state.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  render();
}

function pause() {
  state.running = false;
  cancelAnimationFrame_(state.rafId);
  render();
}

function cancelAnimationFrame_(id) { if (id) cancelAnimationFrame(id); }

function toggleStartPause() {
  state.running ? pause() : start();
}

function reset() {
  pause();
  state.remainingMs = state.totalSeconds * 1000;
  render();
}

function skip() {
  pause();
  advanceMode(false);
}

// ---------- Mode logic ----------
function durationFor(mode) {
  const mins = mode === "work"    ? +els.work.value
             : mode === "break"   ? +els.brk.value
             : +els.long.value;
  return Math.max(1, +mins || 1) * 60;
}

function applyMode(mode) {
  state.mode = mode;
  state.totalSeconds = durationFor(mode);
  state.remainingMs = state.totalSeconds * 1000;
  pause();
  render();
}

function advanceMode(finishedRealSession) {
  if (state.mode === "work") {
    if (finishedRealSession) state.completedSessions++;
    const interval = Math.max(2, +els.longInterval.value || 4);
    const isLong = state.completedSessions > 0 &&
                   state.completedSessions % interval === 0;
    applyMode(isLong ? "longBreak" : "break");
  } else {
    applyMode("work");
  }
  if (els.autoStart.checked) start();
}

function intervalFinished() {
  state.running = false;
  alert_();
  advanceMode(true);
  render();
}

// ---------- Alerts ----------
function alert_() {
  // 1. Visual flash
  document.body.classList.remove("flash");
  els.app.classList.remove("flash");
  void document.body.offsetWidth; // restart animation
  document.body.classList.add("flash");
  els.app.classList.add ? null : null;
  els.app.classList.add("flash");
  setTimeout(() => {
    document.body.classList.remove("flash");
    els.app.classList.remove("flash");
  }, 4200);

  // 2. Blink title
  let visible = true;
  const original = document.title;
  const label = state.mode === "work" ? "🔔 WORK TIME" : "🌿 BREAK TIME";
  const blinker = setInterval(() => {
    document.title = visible ? label : original;
    visible = !visible;
  }, 700);
  setTimeout(() => { document.title = original; clearInterval(blinker); }, 5000);

  // 3. Beep (Web Audio, no files needed)
  playBeep();

  // 4. Notification (asked for only once, on first completion)
  if ("Notification" in window && Notification.permission === "granted") {
    new Notification(label);
  } else if ("Notification" in window && Notification.permission === "default") {
    Notification.requestPermission();
  }
}

let audioCtx;
function playBeep() {
  try {
    audioCtx = audioCtx || new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const now = audioCtx.currentTime;
    [0, .25, .5].forEach(offset => {
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.connect(gain).connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.frequency.value = 880;
      gain.gain.setValue-at-time(0.0001, now + offset);
      osc.start(now + offset);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValue-at-time(0.0001, now + offset + .2);
      osc.stop(now + offset + .22);
    });
  } catch (e) { /* audio blocked until user interaction; harmless */ }
}

// ---------- Render ----------
function fmt(ms) {
  const total = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  const m = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
  const s = String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${m}:${s}`;
}

function render() {
  els.time.textContent = fmt(state.remainingMs);

  const fraction = state.totalSeconds * 1000
    ? state.remainingMs / (state.totalSeconds * 1000) : 0;
  els.ring.style.strokeDashoffset = CIRCUMFERENCE * (1 - fraction);

  const isBreak = state.mode !== "work";
  els.ring.classList.toggle("break", isBreak);
  els.badge.classList.toggle("break", isBreak);
  els.startPause.classList.toggle("break", isBreak);

  els.badge.textContent =
    state.mode === "work" ? "Work" :
    state.mode === "break" ? "Break" : "Long Break";

  els.startPause.textContent = state.running ? "Pause"
    : state.remainingMs < state.totalSeconds * 1000 ? "Resume" : "Start";

  els.sessions.textContent =
    `${state.completedSessions} pomodoro${state.completedSessions === 1 ? "" : "s"} completed`;

  document.title = state.running
    ? `${fmt(state.remainingMs)} — ${els.badge.textContent}`
    : "Pomodoro Timer";
}

// ---------- Events ----------
els.startPause.addEventListener("click", () => {
  playBeep(); // warm up audio context on user gesture
  toggleStartPause();
});
els.skip.addEventListener("click", skip);
els.reset.addEventListener("click", reset);

[els.work, els.brk, els.long].forEach(input => {
  input.addEventListener("change", () => {
    // Apply new duration to the current interval if it hasn't started
    if (!state.running && state.remainingMs === state.totalSeconds * 1000) {
      state.totalSeconds = durationFor(state.mode);
      state.remainingMs = state.totalSeconds * 1000;
      render();
    }
  });
});

// Keyboard shortcuts: Space = start/pause, R = reset
document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT") return;
  if (e.code === "Space") { e.preventDefault(); toggleStartPause(); }
  if (e.key.toLowerCase() === "r") reset();
});

// ---------- Init ----------
applyMode("work");
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

## How the requirements are covered

| Requirement | Implementation |
|---|---|
| **Configurable intervals** | Settings panel with work, short break, long break, and long-break frequency. Changes to an untouched interval apply instantly; otherwise they apply next cycle. |
| **Pause / resume** | One button cycles Start → Pause → Resume. Timing is **timestamp-based** (`performance.now()` + `requestAnimationFrame`), so pausing or background-tab throttling never causes drift. |
| **Reset** | Restores the current interval's full duration; **Skip** jumps to the next phase. |
| **Visual alerts** | Screen flash animation, card "pop" pulse, blinking `document.title`, plus a Web Audio beep and optional browser notification. |
| **Responsive UI** | `clamp()` font sizing, `min(260px, 70vw)` timer ring, flex-wrapping controls, and a mobile media query. |

## Nice extras included

- **Progress ring** — SVG stroke-dashoffset animates down as time elapses, recoloring red→green per mode.
- **Session counter** and automatic long-break every *N* pomodoros.
- **Tab title countdown** so the timer is visible in the browser tab.
- **Keyboard shortcuts** — `Space` toggles, `R` resets.
- **Optional auto-start** for the next interval.

debugging/javascript-debounce
5.00 2026-09-12T05:44:47.510Z · batch 2026-09-12T05-38-13-691Z-hweqg8 · 1349ms · 90 in / 1444 out tokens · $0.0011 · stop: stop

Output

The bug is that the debounce never cancels the previous timer:

- `timeout` is declared but never assigned.
- `clearTimeout(timeout)` is never called.
- Every call to the returned function schedules a new `setTimeout`, so every call eventually invokes `fn`.

So instead of “only the last call within `delay` runs,” every call runs after `delay`.

Safe rewrite:

```js
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeoutId;

  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      fn.apply(this, args);
    }, delay);
  };
}
```

Why this works:

- `clearTimeout(timeoutId)` cancels any pending invocation.
- `timeoutId = setTimeout(...)` stores the new timer.
- Rest args `...args` captures the arguments safely.
- `fn.apply(this, args)` preserves the original `this` context.

Optional safer version with a `cancel` method:

```js
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeoutId = null;

  function debounced(...args) {
    if (timeoutId !== null) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }

    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      timeoutId = null;
      fn.apply(this, args);
    }, delay);
  }

  debounced.cancel = function () {
    if (timeoutId !== null) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      timeoutId = null;
    }
  };

  return debounced;
}
```

The key fix is: always cancel the previous timer before scheduling the next one.

Judge judge:openrouter-glm-5.2: 5

Correct diagnosis, clear explanation, and robust replacement.
  • 5 correctness: Identifies missing clearTimeout and explains why calls stack up.
  • 5 explanation-quality: Explains the bug's cause before presenting the fix.
  • 5 code-quality: Fix preserves this/arguments and stays a debounce, not a throttle rewrite.

weighted score: 5.00
5.00 2026-09-12T05:41:33.164Z · batch 2026-09-12T05-38-13-691Z-hweqg8 · 4319ms · 66 in / 841 out tokens · $0.0032 · stop: stop

Output

### What is Wrong

The purpose of a debounce function is to reset a timer every time an event occurs, ensuring the target function `fn` only executes after a period of inactivity.

In your current code:
1. **The timer is never cleared:** `clearTimeout(timeout)` is never called.
2. **The timer ID is never saved:** The result of `setTimeout(...)` is never assigned to `timeout`.

Because previous timers are never cancelled, calling the debounced function multiple times in quick succession simply schedules multiple independent timers. When their delays expire, **every single one of them will fire**, defeating the purpose of debouncing.

---

### Safe Rewrite

Here is the corrected and modernized version using ES6 rest parameters (`...args`):

```javascript
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeoutId;

  return function(...args) {
    // 1. Cancel the previous timer if the function is called again
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    // 2. Schedule a new timer and save its ID
    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      fn.apply(this, args);
    }, delay);
  };
}
```

### Key Improvements:
- **`clearTimeout(timeoutId)`:** Cancels any pending execution before setting a new one.
- **`timeoutId = setTimeout(...)`:** Tracks the active timer so it can be cleared on subsequent calls.
- **`...args` (Rest parameters):** Cleaner and more reliable than accessing the legacy `arguments` object.
- **Arrow function preserves `this`:** The arrow function correctly retains the `this` context from the outer wrapper function.

Judge judge:openrouter-glm-5.2: 5

Correctly identifies missing timer cancellation and never saving the timeout ID, explains why calls stack up, and provides a robust rewrite preserving this/arguments.
  • 5 correctness: Diagnoses missing clearTimeout and unsaved timer ID; explains stacking of independent timers.
  • 5 explanation-quality: Clear cause explanation before fix, enumerating the two concrete bugs.
  • 5 code-quality: Rewrite preserves this via arrow function and args via rest params, remains a debounce.

5.00 2026-09-12T06:04:25.482Z · batch 2026-09-12T05-38-13-691Z-hweqg8 · 607ms · 69 in / 1433 out tokens · $0.0004 · stop: stop

Output

## What's wrong

The `timeout` variable is declared but **never assigned or cleared**. Every call to the returned function calls `setTimeout`, which schedules an *independent* timer — and nothing ever cancels the previous one.

So if the user types 5 characters in quick succession, you get 5 scheduled timers, and `fn` fires 5 times. That's the exact opposite of debouncing, which must **cancel the pending call** every time a new one arrives.

Trace of the bug:

```javascript
const sayHi = debounce(() => console.log('hi'), 100);

sayHi(); // schedules timer A
sayHi(); // schedules timer B — A is never cancelled!
// ~100ms later: logs "hi" twice
```

## Fixed version

Two things are required, and both were missing: capture the timer ID from `setTimeout`, and `clearTimeout` at the top of each invocation.

```javascript
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeoutId;
  return function(...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId); // cancel any pending call (safe even if undefined)
    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      fn.apply(this, args);
    }, delay);               // store the new timer's ID
  };
}
```

Now:

```javascript
sayHi(); // schedules timer A
sayHi(); // cancels A, schedules B
// ~100ms later: logs "hi" once ✓
```

## Notes

- `clearTimeout(undefined)` is a harmless no-op, so calling it before any timer exists is safe.
- Your original `arguments` inside the arrow function actually worked correctly (arrow functions inherit the outer function's `arguments`), but rest params (`...args`) are the modern, clearer style.
- If you need a hard guarantee against leaks (e.g., in components that unmount), consider also exposing a `cancel()` method that calls `clearTimeout(timeoutId)` — otherwise a pending call will still fire after you stop using the debounced function.

Judge judge:openrouter-glm-5.2: 5

Correctly diagnoses the missing clearTimeout and explains why calls stack up, provides a robust replacement preserving this/arguments, and adds helpful notes without overcomplicating.
  • 5 correctness: Identifies that timeout is never assigned/cleared, explains stacking timers, and uses clearTimeout before scheduling a new timer.
  • 5 explanation-quality: Clear cause-before-fix explanation with a concrete trace showing multiple timers firing, plus notes on edge cases.
  • 5 code-quality: Fixed debounce preserves this via normal function and args via rest params, stores timer ID, and remains a debounce rather than a throttle.

5.00 2026-09-12T05:41:31.184Z · batch 2026-09-12T05-38-13-691Z-hweqg8 · 870ms · 124 in / 4008 out tokens · $0.0019 · stop: stop

Output

The problem is that the function never cancels the previous timer.

```js
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeout;
  return function() {
    setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, arguments), delay);
  };
}
```

Every time the returned function is called, it creates a brand-new `setTimeout` and then forgets about it. `timeout` is never assigned, and no previous timeout is cleared. So instead of debouncing, it just delays every call.

For example, with this:

```js
const debounced = debounce(console.log, 300);

debounced("a");
debounced("b");
debounced("c");
```

the original version schedules three separate timers, so `"a"`, `"b"`, and `"c"` may all fire. A real debounce should only fire the last call after the user stops for `delay` milliseconds.

A minimal fixed version is:

```js
function debounce(fn, delay) {
  let timeout;

  return function () {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, arguments), delay);
  };
}
```

Now each call clears the pending timer before scheduling a new one, so only the latest call remains.

A safer and slightly more complete version would expose a `cancel()` method and use rest parameters:

```js
function debounce(fn, delay = 0) {
  let timerId = null;

  const debounced = function (...args) {
    if (timerId !== null) {
      clearTimeout(timerId);
    }

    timerId = setTimeout(() => {
      timerId = null;
      fn.apply(this, args);
    }, delay);
  };

  debounced.cancel = function () {
    if (timerId !== null) {
      clearTimeout(timerId);
      timerId = null;
    }
  };

  return debounced;
}
```

Usage:

```js
const handleInput = debounce(function (event) {
  console.log("searching for:", this.query);
}, 300);

handleInput();

// If you need to stop a pending debounced call:
handleInput.cancel();
```

The key fix is: store the timeout ID and call `clearTimeout()` on every new invocation.

Judge judge:openrouter-glm-5.2: 5

Correctly diagnoses the missing clearTimeout, explains why calls stack up, and provides a robust fixed debounce preserving this/arguments.
  • 5 correctness: Identifies that timeout is never assigned/cleared, causing stacked timers, and fixes it with clearTimeout.
  • 5 explanation-quality: Explains the bug clearly before the fix, with a concrete example showing multiple firings.
  • 5 code-quality: Minimal fix preserves this/arguments and stays a debounce; optional enhanced version adds cancel and rest params without changing behavior.

Output

**Rubric: Evaluating a 3-Day San Francisco City Break Plan**

**Scale:** 0–4 per criterion.  
0 = missing/harmful · 1 = poor · 2 = adequate · 3 = good · 4 = excellent  
**Suggested weights:** Correctness 25% · Completeness 25% · Assumptions 15% · Usability 20% · Risk 15%

---

### 1. Correctness — 25%
*Factual accuracy and real-world feasibility.*

- **4:** All SF-specific details are accurate and feasible: neighborhoods, venues, transit modes/routes, walking times, hills, and rainy-day options. Geographic clustering is sensible, e.g., Ferry Building + Embarcadero + North Beach; Golden Gate Park + Inner Sunset; Mission + Hayes Valley. Transit advice is realistic: Muni, BART, cable car, ferry, Clipper card, hill/fog considerations.
- **3:** Mostly accurate; minor outdated or imprecise details that would not derail the trip.
- **2:** Several inaccuracies or vague claims; some venues, transit, or walking times are questionable.
- **1:** Major errors—wrong transit, closed venue, impossible walking route—that would disrupt the trip.
- **0:** Hallucinated, unsafe, or nonsensical recommendations.

---

### 2. Completeness — 25%
*Coverage of every prompt requirement.*

- **4:** Fully covers all 3 days and includes food, walking, bookstores, scenic views, and low-key nightlife. Includes neighborhoods, daily pacing, transit notes, rainy-day alternatives, and explicitly avoids overpacking. Gives specific examples and backup options.
- **3:** Covers almost all requirements; one minor element is thin—e.g., low-key nightlife is mentioned but not developed.
- **2:** Misses at least one major requirement—e.g., no rainy-day alternatives or no transit notes—or is too generic.
- **1:** Misses multiple major requirements; reads as an attraction list, not a plan.
- **0:** Does not address the prompt.

---

### 3. Assumptions — 15%
*Explicit, reasonable assumptions and adaptability.*

- **4:** States or clearly labels key assumptions: lodging area, start/end times, budget, mobility, weather, dietary needs, transit familiarity. Plan stays adaptable if assumptions change.
- **3:** Some assumptions stated; reasonable defaults used. Minor hidden assumptions.
- **2:** Few assumptions stated; relies on unstated assumptions—e.g., unlimited budget, high energy, no mobility limits.
- **1:** Major unstated assumptions make the plan unsuitable for many travelers.
- **0:** No awareness of assumptions; rigid and prescriptive.

---

### 4. Usability — 20%
*Actionability, clarity, and pacing.*

- **4:** Clear day-by-day structure, time blocks, neighborhood clustering, transit directions, reservation tips, and practical notes. Balanced pacing; easy to execute without re-planning.
- **3:** Mostly clear and actionable; minor gaps in logistics or pacing.
- **2:** Some structure but hard to execute; lacks transit details, timing, or logical flow.
- **1:** Disorganized or vague; traveler would need to rebuild it.
- **0:** Unusable.

---

### 5. Risk — 15%
*Risk identification and mitigation.*

- **4:** Identifies and mitigates key risks: rain/fog, transit delays, hills/fatigue, crowds, closures, safety, reservations, costs, accessibility. Provides rainy-day alternatives and flexible backups. Avoids risky advice.
- **3:** Mentions most risks and some mitigations; minor gaps.
- **2:** Acknowledges one or two risks but lacks concrete mitigations.
- **1:** Ignores major risks or gives risky advice—over-scheduling, unsafe night walks, ignoring weather.
- **0:** Actively unsafe or reckless.

---

### Red Flags / Automatic Deductions
- Overpacked schedule, e.g., 6+ major stops/day with no buffers, contradicts the prompt.
- No rainy-day alternatives.
- No transit notes.
- Factual errors about SF geography or transit.
- Low-key nightlife replaced with loud clubs.
- Walking plan ignores hills, fog, or mobility needs.

---

### Scoring Guide
- **18–20:** Excellent — ready to use with minor tweaks.
- **14–17:** Good — useful, needs small corrections.
- **10–13:** Adequate — covers basics but gaps limit usability.
- **5–9:** Poor — major omissions or errors.
- **0–4:** Unacceptable — fails the prompt.

# Evaluation Rubric: 3-Day San Francisco Itinerary

**Scoring Scale per Category:**
* **4 – Exemplary:** Meets all requirements with depth, nuance, and superior local context.
* **3 – Proficient:** Meets all key requirements accurately with minor gaps.
* **2 – Developing:** Misses some requirements, has minor geographic/logistical errors, or exhibits poor pacing.
* **1 – Inadequate:** Fails to meet prompt constraints, contains significant factual errors, or is unusable.

---

### 1. Correctness (Geographic & Logistical Accuracy)
*Evaluates whether the physical geography, transit options, venues, and walking routes reflect real-world San Francisco.*

| Score | Criteria |
| :--- | :--- |
| **4** | Venues are real, currently open, and properly located. Neighborhood groupings make spatial sense (e.g., North Beach pairs with Telegraph Hill/Chinatown; Inner Sunset pairs with Golden Gate Park). Transit lines cited (Muni bus/metro, BART, cable cars) actually serve the recommended routes. Walking paths account for SF’s severe topography. |
| **3** | Venues exist and pairings make general sense. Transit suggestions are plausible, though specific line numbers may be omitted. Minimal backtrack walking. |
| **2** | Includes notable geographic friction (e.g., asking the traveler to cross the city back and forth in a single afternoon, or suggesting unrealistic walking routes up 30% grades). May reference closed venues. |
| **1** | Severe hallucinations (invented places, impossible transit, wildly inaccurate geography like walking from the Ferry Building to Ocean Beach for lunch). |

---

### 2. Completeness (Coverage of Core Constraints)
*Evaluates whether every element requested in the prompt is explicitly included.*

*Checklist of required items:*
- [ ] 3-day structure
- [ ] Specific neighborhoods highlighted
- [ ] Incorporates **Food**
- [ ] Incorporates **Scenic Views**
- [ ] Incorporates **Bookstores** (named independent shops)
- [ ] Incorporates **Walking**
- [ ] Incorporates **Low-key nightlife** (e.g., quiet cocktail bars, acoustic music, evening dessert/cafes; *not* loud clubs)
- [ ] Transit notes (public transit, ride-hailing guidance)
- [ ] Rainy-day alternatives
- [ ] Relaxed pacing (not over-scheduled)

| Score | Criteria |
| :--- | :--- |
| **4** | Every checklist item is fully integrated into each day naturally. Bookstores are named landmarks (e.g., City Lights, Green Apple, Booksmith). Rainy-day options are concrete SF indoor activities (e.g., SFMOMA, Exploratorium, Cal Academy, indoor markets). |
| **3** | All checklist items are present, but one or two feel like an afterthought (e.g., rainy-day alternatives are listed in a generic bulleted list at the end rather than integrated into the daily plans). |
| **2** | Missing 1–2 key requirements (e.g., forgot bookstores, omitted transit advice, or missed low-key nightlife). |
| **1** | Missing 3 or more core requirements. |

---

### 3. Assumptions & Context Handling
*Evaluates how well the response handles unstated traveler variables (budget, base location, physical ability, arrival/departure).*

| Score | Criteria |
| :--- | :--- |
| **4** | Explicitly states sensible baseline assumptions (e.g., assumes a centrally located base like Hayes Valley, the Mission, or Union Square; assumes moderate fitness for hills). Highlights trade-offs if the traveler has different preferences (e.g., budget levels, mobility limitations). |
| **3** | Operates on reasonable implied assumptions without explicitly detailing them, but the itinerary remains viable for a typical solo traveler or couple. |
| **2** | Relies on unstated, extreme assumptions (e.g., assumes access to a rental car despite the walking focus, or assumes an unlimited fine-dining budget without indication). |
| **1** | Demonstrates no awareness of traveler context; recommends contradictory or impossible timelines without context. |

---

### 4. Usability & Pacing
*Evaluates organization, readability, and adherence to the "avoid packing too tightly" constraint.*

| Score | Criteria |
| :--- | :--- |
| **4** | **Pacing:** Maximum of 2–3 anchor activities per day with dedicated buffer time for wandering.  <br>**Structure:** Logical chronological flow (Morning / Afternoon / Evening). Clear headings, concise descriptions, and actionable transit transitions between neighborhoods. |
| **3** | Well-organized and readable. Pacing is mostly relaxed, though one day might feel slightly brisk or transit-heavy. |
| **2** | Schedule is noticeably rushed (4+ activities per day requiring strict timekeeping), directly violating the prompt's pacing constraint. Structure is dense or hard to scan on mobile. |
| **1** | Overwhelming list of activities with no daily structure, or an extreme rush that would exhaust the traveler. |

---

### 5. Risk, Safety & Contingency
*Evaluates local safety considerations, environmental factors, and contingency management.*

| Score | Criteria |
| :--- | :--- |
| **4** | **Microclimates/Weather:** Warns about fog ("Karl"), rapid temperature drops, and the necessity of layers.  <br>**Safety:** Steers clear of known high-property-crime or sensitive corridors (e.g., Tenderloin, Mid-Market late at night) or provides polite, practical advice. Mentions car break-in risks if driving.  <br>**Contingency:** Notes when reservations are essential (popular dining/views) and offers clear wet-weather pivots. |
| **3** | Mentions weather/layers and offers basic contingency notes (reservations/rain). Avoids dangerous recommendations. |
| **2** | Ignores local climate realities (e.g., suggesting beach sunbathing or failing to mention fog/cold winds). Sends travelers through high-risk areas at night without caution. |
| **1** | Suggests unsafe behaviors (e.g., leaving luggage visible in a car, walking through high-crime areas late at night) or fails completely on environmental realities. |

---

## Scoring Summary & Pass/Fail Threshold

* **Target Score:** 17–20 / 20 (High-quality, ready to use)
* **Acceptable Score:** 14–16 / 20 (Minor revisions required)
* **Fail Threshold:** $\le 13$ / 20 or a score of **1** in any individual category.

# Evaluation Rubric: 3-Day San Francisco City Break Itinerary

**Prompt under evaluation:**
> "Plan a 3-day city break to San Francisco for someone who likes food, walking, bookstores, scenic views, and low-key nightlife. Include neighborhoods, daily pacing, transit notes, and rainy-day alternatives. Avoid packing the schedule too tightly."

**How to use:** Read the full response once. Run the completeness checklist first, then spot-check facts. Score out of 100 across five criteria. *Examples below are illustrative, not required content — verify venue status at evaluation time, as SF businesses turn over quickly.*

| Criterion | Points | Core question |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Correctness | 25 | Are places, transit, and logistics real, accurate, and geographically sane? |
| 2. Completeness | 25 | Does it cover every required element and all five interests? |
| 3. Assumptions | 15 | Are assumptions explicit, minimal, and reasonable? |
| 4. Usability | 20 | Could the traveler execute it as written, without extra research? |
| 5. Risk | 15 | Does the plan avoid hazards and flag the ones it can't? |

---

## 1. Correctness (25 pts)

*Spot-verify 3–5 named venues and all transit claims; test whether each day's geography hangs together.*

- **Excellent (22–25):** Venues, viewpoints, and transit options are real and plausibly current; days cluster by neighborhood so travel claims hold up; walking times are honest (Haight → Golden Gate Park: minutes; Mission → Fisherman's Wharf: a haul); "low-key nightlife" is actually low-key; SF-specific realities are right (cable car lines, hills, microclimates).
- **Good (17–21):** Mostly accurate; 1–2 minor slips (generous walking time, hours stated without hedging); geography sound.
- **Fair (11–16):** Several errors — transit hop described as "quick" that takes 45 minutes; venues placed in wrong neighborhoods; a day's sequence impossible on its own stated timeline.
- **Poor (0–10):** Fabricated or clearly closed venues/lines; scrambled geography; misreads the brief (nightclub crawl for "low-key," rental car for a walk-focused trip).

## 2. Completeness (25 pts)

*Confirm each required element is present and substantive:*

- ☐ Three clearly delineated days
- ☐ Neighborhoods named and used to organize each day
- ☐ Pacing designed in (2–3 anchors/day, buffer time) — honors "not too tightly packed"
- ☐ Transit notes (modes, when walking beats transit, payment)
- ☐ Rainy-day alternatives that are genuinely indoor (not "bring an umbrella")
- ☐ All five interests served: food, walking, bookstores, scenic views, low-key nightlife
- ☐ Meals/coffee covered, or explicitly scoped out with a reason

- **Excellent (22–25):** All boxes checked and integrated — rain swaps attached to the specific days they'd interrupt; pacing is structural (optional add-ons, not rigid cramming).
- **Good (17–21):** All major elements present; pacing mentioned but thin; rain plan generic ("visit museums").
- **Fair (11–16):** One or two elements missing or token (a single bookstore name-dropped in passing); rain plan generic; pacing ignored.
- **Poor (0–10):** Multiple elements missing; a highlights list rather than a plan.

## 3. Assumptions (15 pts)

- **Excellent (12–15):** Short, explicit assumption set up front (season, dates, home base, budget tier, walking tolerance); assumptions are the minimum needed; volatile facts (hours, fares, reservation lead times) hedged with "verify"; notes how the plan flexes if an assumption doesn't hold.
- **Good (9–11):** Some assumptions named; material ones unstated but benign; light hedging.
- **Fair (5–8):** Heavy unstated assumptions steering the plan (fixed budget, assumed car, fitness level); or invents constraints the prompt never gave and builds around them.
- **Poor (0–4):** No acknowledgment — reads as universal truth — or drowns the plan in invented detail.

## 4. Usability (20 pts)

- **Excellent (16–20):** Scannable day blocks; every stop has name + neighborhood + transit access; realistic durations including travel time between stops; built-in swaps ("if the line is long, do X instead"); executable with no further research; length proportionate.
- **Good (12–15):** Clear structure, mostly specific; some stops vague ("explore Hayes Valley") or missing transit detail.
- **Fair (7–11):** Dense or disorganized; times don't add up; reader must reconstruct logistics; over-scheduled despite the brief.
- **Poor (0–6):** Unstructured, internally contradictory, or unusable without rewriting.

## 5. Risk (15 pts)

*Score both risk avoidance (the plan itself) and risk communication (flags + mitigations). The bar is proportionate — real risks named without fear-mongering, no false assurance.*

- **Excellent (12–15):** SF-specific risks handled: car break-in warning if any parking involved; caution on specific blocks at night without hyperbole; steep-hill reality after a long walking day; fog can erase headline views (time-of-day advice or backup); book-ahead items flagged with lead times; no single point of failure.
- **Good (9–11):** One or two real risks flagged; misses booking lead times or night-walking nuance; otherwise sound.
- **Fair (5–8):** Plan creates risk: tight same-day chains that collapse if one stop slips; late-night routing through areas it elsewhere calls dicey; treats weather as irrelevant.
- **Poor (0–4):** Unfounded fear-mongering that guts the trip, or false certainty ("you'll definitely see X") paired with fragile logistics; nothing hedged.

---

## Automatic caps (fatal flaws)

Apply before summing:
- Fabricated venue or transit line → **Correctness capped at Fair**
- No rainy-day alternatives → **Completeness capped at Fair**
- Schedule requires ~5+ major stops/day or 12+ hours out (ignores the pacing constraint) → **overall capped at 74**
- Recommends a rental car for this walk-focused brief → **Correctness capped at Fair**

## Bonus (up to +3, total capped at 100)

- Microclimate-aware view timing (e.g., fog logic) (+1)
- Neighborhood clustering that visibly cuts transit time (+1)
- Cost signals per stop or per day (+1)

## Score interpretation

- **90–100:** Ready to hand to the traveler
- **75–89:** Solid; verify hours/fares and add a swap or two before use
- **60–74:** Usable skeleton; needs fact-checking and repacing
- **<60:** Unreliable without major rework

**60-second evaluator check:** (1) Any made-up place or line? (2) All required components and five interests present? (3) Could I follow Day 2 without Googling anything? (4) Do the times add up with honest travel time? (5) Assumptions stated and risks proportionately flagged?
